{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))\leq{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\operatorname{L}^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
. The only difference is that if
\psi_{\lambda}
is known to be a
\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
-bimodule map, then so will be
\varphi_{\lambda}
.

Assume that
(\varphi_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
is a net of finite rank maps in
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
with CB norm bounded by
\|\varphi_{\lambda}\|_{cb}\leq{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
, assumed to be finite. As in the previous paragraph, there are
a_{\lambda}\in\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
such that
\varphi_{\lambda}=\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})\mathord{\upharpoonright}_{\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}
. Then
\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})
are normal, finite rank, CB maps in
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB^{\sigma}(\operatorname{L}^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
with
\|\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})\|_{cb}=\|\varphi_{\lambda}\|_{cb}
. Take
x\in\operatorname{L}^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\setminus\{0\},\omega\in\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\setminus\{0\}
and
\varepsilon>0
. Since products are linearly dense in
\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
(
[
7
, Section 3]
), we can find
\omega_{k},\omega^{\prime}_{k}\in\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\,(1\leq k\leq K)
such that
\|\omega-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\omega_{k}\star\omega^{\prime}_{k}\|\leq\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2\|x\|(1+{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})))}
. Furthermore, for any
k
we have
\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x\in\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
(
[
10
, Lemma 4.6]
), hence there is
\lambda_{0}\in\Lambda
such that

\|\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})(\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x)-\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x\|\leq\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2K(1+\|\omega_{k}\|)}\quad(1\leq k\leq K,\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}).

For
\lambda\geq\lambda_{0}
we have

\begin{split}&\quad\;|\langle\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})(x)-x,\omega\rangle|\\
&\leq\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\langle\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})(x)-x,\omega_{k}\star\omega^{\prime}_{k}\rangle|=\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\langle\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})(x)-\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x,\omega_{k}\rangle|\\
&=\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\langle\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})(\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x)-\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x,\omega_{k}\rangle|\leq\tfrac{\varepsilon}{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})(\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x)-\omega^{\prime}_{k}\star x\|\|\omega_{k}\|\leq\varepsilon.\end{split}

This proves
\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})(x)\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}x
weak
∗
and consequently
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\operatorname{L}^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))\leq{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
. As above, a minor modification gives
\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
-bimodule version.
∎

Because of Proposition
3.3
, we will focus on
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
and
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
. Let us now recall Cowling-Haagerup constant and its central variant for discrete quantum groups (
[
3
,
9
,
14
]
).

Definition 3.4
.

Let

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

be a discrete quantum group.

•

The Cowling-Haagerup constant of

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

,
\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\in\left[1,+\infty\right]
, is the infimum of all numbers
C\geq 1
such that there is a net
(a_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
in
\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
with
\|a_{\lambda}\|_{cb}\leq C
and
a_{\lambda}\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}\mathbbm{1}
pointwise. If no such number exists, set
\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})=+\infty
. If
\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})<+\infty
, one says that

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

is weakly amenable.

•

The central Cowling-Haagerup constant
\mathcal{Z}\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\in\left[1,+\infty\right]
is the infimum of all numbers
C\geq 1
such that there is a net
(a_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
in
\mathcal{Z}\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
with
\|a_{\lambda}\|_{cb}\leq C
and
a_{\lambda}\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}\mathbbm{1}
pointwise. If no such number exists, set
\mathcal{Z}\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})=+\infty
. If
\mathcal{Z}\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})<+\infty
, one says that

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

is centrally weakly amenable.

Similarly to Definition
3.1
, the infimum is actually attainable. Let us note that in the definition of
\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
we could also consider more general nets
(a_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
assumed only to be in the Fourier algebra
\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. A standard approximation argument shows that both definitions are equivalent (see e.g.
[
3
, Section 3.2]
). Instead of speaking about pointwise convergence, one can require that
(a_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
forms an approximate identity in the Fourier algebra
\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. In this context, both conditions are equivalent. The following result provides a link between Cowling-Haagerup constant of a discrete quantum group

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

and the associated module
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Its proof is quite standard, compare e.g.
[
3
, Theorem 6.6]
.

Theorem 3.5
.

For any discrete quantum group

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})={}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))\quad\textnormal{ and }\quad\mathcal{Z}\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})={}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})).

Proof.

Assume
\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})<+\infty
and let
(a_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
be a net in
\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
such that
\|a_{\lambda}\|_{cb}\leq\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
and
a_{\lambda}\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}\mathbbm{1}
pointwise. Since
\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\subseteq\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
, we can find normal functionals
\omega_{\lambda}\in\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
such that
a_{\lambda}=\widehat{\lambda}(\omega_{\lambda})
. Then
\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})=(\omega_{\lambda}\otimes\textnormal{id})\widehat{\Delta}
, consider
\varphi_{\lambda}=\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})\mathord{\upharpoonright}_{\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}
. This map is of finite rank, belongs to
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
and has CB norm equal to
\|a_{\lambda}\|_{cb}
. Furthermore, since
\sup_{\lambda\in\Lambda}\|a_{\lambda}\|_{cb}<\infty
, to see that
\varphi_{\lambda}\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}\textnormal{id}
in the point-norm topology of
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
, it is enough to look at the dense subspace
\Pol(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Since
a_{\lambda}\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}\mathbbm{1}
pointwise, for any
\alpha\in\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}),1\leq i,j\leq\dim(\alpha)
we have
\omega_{\lambda}(U^{\alpha}_{i,j})\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}\delta_{i,j}
and consequently
\varphi_{\lambda}(U^{\alpha}_{i,j})=\sum_{k=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}\omega_{\lambda}(U^{\alpha}_{i,k})U^{\alpha}_{k,j}
converges in norm to
U^{\alpha}_{i,j}
. We conclude that
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))\leq\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
.

Assume now that
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))<+\infty
with the corresponding net
(\varphi_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
in
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
. As discussed in Section
2
, there is a multiplier
a_{\lambda}\in\M^{l}_{cb}(\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
such that
\varphi_{\lambda}=\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})\mathord{\upharpoonright}_{\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}
, in particular
\|a_{\lambda}\|_{cb}=\|\varphi_{\lambda}\|_{cb}
. Since
\varphi_{\lambda}
is of finite rank, we have in fact
a_{\lambda}\in\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. As the net
(\varphi_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
converges to
id
in the point-norm topology, we have
a_{\lambda}\xrightarrow[\lambda\in\Lambda]{}\mathbbm{1}
pointwise. This shows
\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\leq{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
.

The central and bimodule variant is proved in a similar way, with slight modification. In the first direction, we additionally have
a_{\lambda}\in\mathcal{Z}\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
, then
\Theta^{l}(a_{\lambda})\mathord{\upharpoonright}_{\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}
is a
\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
-bimodule map giving
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))\leq\mathcal{Z}\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Conversely since
\varphi_{\lambda}
is a map of bimodules,
a_{\lambda}
is central.
∎

Remark 3.6
.

It is an interesting question whether
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))={}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
always holds, equivalently (by Theorem
3.5
) whether Cowling-Haagerup constant of

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

is equal to its central variant
\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})=\mathcal{Z}\Lambda_{cb}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. To the best of our knowledge, no counterexample is known. An analogous result for strong amenability is false (see e.g.
[
9
, Theorem 7.6]
).

4.
Cowling-Haagerup constant of the product

In this section we prove our main result: (central) Cowling-Haagerup constant of discrete quantum groups is multiplicative (Theorem
4.8
). We will do this by establishing first an analogous result for modules
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
(Proposition
4.6
) and then using Theorem
3.5
. As mentioned in the introduction, our proof of Proposition
4.6
is a modification of the proof of
[
4
, Theorem 12.3.13]
(see also Remark
4.7
).

It will be convenient to work in the more general language of completely contractive Banach algebras and operator modules, see Section
2
. The next lemma is a bimodule generalisation of
[
4
, Lemma 12.3.16]
. Recall that any
A
-
B
-bimodule, is also a right
A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B
-module (see Section
2
).

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
A,B
be completely contractive Banach algebras,
X
an
A
-
B
-bimodule and
F\subseteq E\subseteq X
finite dimensional submodules. Take
C\geq 1
. The following statements are equivalent:

(1)

there is
\varphi\in\CB_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}(X,E)
such that
\varphi(x)=x\,(x\in F)
and
\|\varphi\|_{\CB(X,E)}\leq C
,

(2)

|\kappa(u)|\leq C\|q(u)\|
for
u\in F\odot E^{*}
, where
\kappa\colon E\odot E^{*}\rightarrow\mathbb{C}
is the pairing map and
q\colon X\widehat{\otimes}E^{*}\rightarrow X\widehat{\otimes}_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}E^{*}
is the canonical quotient map.

Proof.

Assume that we have
\varphi\in\CB_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}(X,E)
as in
(1)
, take
u\in F\odot E^{*}
and write
u=\sum_{k=1}^{n}x_{k}\otimes\omega_{k}
for some
x_{k}\in F,\omega_{k}\in E^{*}
. Then using identifications
\CB(F,E)=\CB(F,E^{**})\simeq(F\widehat{\otimes}E^{*})^{*}
and
\CB_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}(X,E)\simeq(X\widehat{\otimes}_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}E^{*})^{*}
we calculate

\begin{split}&\quad\;|\kappa(u)|=\bigl|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\langle\omega_{k},x_{k}\rangle\bigr|=\bigl|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\langle\omega_{k},\varphi(x_{k})\rangle\bigr|=|\langle\varphi,u\rangle_{\CB(X,E),X\widehat{\otimes}E^{*}}|\\
&=|\langle\varphi,q(u)\rangle_{\CB_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}(X,E),X\widehat{\otimes}_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}E^{*}}|\leq C\|q(u)\|,\end{split}

i.e.
(2)
holds.

Conversely, assume that
|\kappa(u)|\leq C\|q(u)\|
for all
u\in F\odot E^{*}
. Then the functional

(4.1)

X\widehat{\otimes}_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}E^{*}\supseteq q(F\odot E^{*})\ni q(u)\mapsto\kappa(u)\in\mathbb{C}

is well defined and has norm bounded by
C
. By Hahn-Banach theorem, we can find
\varphi\in(X\widehat{\otimes}_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}E^{*})^{*}\simeq\CB_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}(X,E)
which extends (
4.1
) and has norm
\leq C
. For
x\in F,\omega\in E^{*}
we have

\langle\omega,\varphi(x)\rangle=\langle\varphi,q(x\otimes\omega)\rangle_{\CB_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}(X,E),X\widehat{\otimes}_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}E^{*}}=\kappa(x\otimes\omega)=\langle\omega,x\rangle

hence
\varphi(x)=x
.
∎

Remark 4.2
.

For general completely contractive Banach algebras
A,B
and bimodules
X,Y
it can happen that
{}_{A}\CB_{B}(X,Y)\subsetneq\CB_{A^{op}\widehat{\otimes}B}(X,Y)
(consider the trivial module structure
ax=0,ay=0
for
a\in A,x\in X,y\in Y
). On the other hand, in what follows we will be interested in the
\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
-bimodule structure on
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
for a discrete quantum group

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

, or its sub-bimodules. In this situation we have equality
{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))=\CB_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})^{op}\widehat{\otimes}\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
. Indeed, this easily follows from considering the dense subspace
\Pol(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\subseteq\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
and the following observation: for
x\in\Pol(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
there exists
\omega\in\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
such that
\omega\star x=x\star\omega=x
.

Next we establish several useful properties of the left
\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
-module
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
.

Lemma 4.3
.

Let

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

be a discrete quantum group and
C\geq 1
. The following conditions are equivalent:

(1)

{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))\leq C
,

(2)

for every
\varepsilon>0
and finite
\emptyset\neq F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
there is a finite rank
\varphi\in{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
such that
\|\varphi(x)-x\|\leq\varepsilon\|x\|\,(x\in\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
and
\|\varphi\|_{cb}\leq C
.

Proof.

(1)\Rightarrow(2)
: take
\varepsilon>0
and finite
\emptyset\neq F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Since
\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
is a finite dimensional normed space with basis
\{U^{\alpha}_{i,j}\,|\,\alpha\in F,\,1\leq i,j\leq\dim(\alpha)\}
we can find
D>0
so that

\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i,j=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}|x^{\alpha}_{i,j}|\leq D\|\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i,j=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}x^{\alpha}_{i,j}U^{\alpha}_{i,j}\|

for all
\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i,j=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}x^{\alpha}_{i,j}U^{\alpha}_{i,j}\in\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. By
(1)
, there is
\varphi\in{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
such that
\|\varphi\|_{cb}\leq C
and

\|\varphi(U^{\alpha}_{i,j})-U^{\alpha}_{i,j}\|\leq\tfrac{\varepsilon}{D}\quad(\alpha\in F,1\leq i,j\leq\dim(\alpha)).

Then for any
x=\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i,j=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}x^{\alpha}_{i,j}U^{\alpha}_{i,j}\in\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
we have

\|\varphi(x)-x\|\leq\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i,j=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}|x^{\alpha}_{i,j}|\|\varphi(U^{\alpha}_{i,j})-U^{\alpha}_{i,j}\|\leq\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i,j=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}|x^{\alpha}_{i,j}|\tfrac{\varepsilon}{D}\leq\varepsilon\|x\|.

(2)\Rightarrow(1)
: for
\varepsilon>0
and finite
\emptyset\neq F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
, let
\varphi_{\varepsilon,F}\in{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\CB(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
be the map from
(2)
. As
\|\varphi_{\varepsilon,F}\|_{cb}\leq C
for all
\varepsilon,F
and
\Pol(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
is norm dense in
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
, it easily follows that net
(\varphi_{\varepsilon,F})_{(\varepsilon,F)}
indexed over
\varepsilon\in\left]0,1\right[
and finite
\emptyset\neq F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
gives
{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))\leq C
.
∎

There is also a natural analog of Lemma
4.3
for
\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
-bimodule
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Recall that for finite
\emptyset\neq F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
,
p_{F}\in\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})\subseteq\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
is the central projection
p_{F}=\sum_{\alpha\in F}p_{\alpha}
.

Lemma 4.4
.

Let

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

be a discrete quantum group and
\varepsilon>0
. For a finite set
\emptyset\neq F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
,

1\leq\|p_{F}\|_{\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\leq\sqrt{\sum_{\alpha\in F}\dim_{q}(\alpha)^{2}}.

Proof.

Recall that
p_{F}=\widehat{\lambda}(\omega_{F})
(equation (
2.1
)). Let
\|x\|_{2}=h(x^{*}x)^{1/2}\,(x\in\LL^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
be the
2
-norm on
\operatorname{L}^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Using orthogonality relations (
[
21
, Theorem 1.4.3]
) we see

\begin{split}\|p_{F}\|_{\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}^{2}=\|\omega_{F}\|^{2}&\leq\bigl\|\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}\dim_{q}(\alpha)\uprho_{\alpha,i}U^{\alpha}_{i,i}\bigr\|_{2}^{2}=\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}\dim_{q}(\alpha)^{2}{\uprho_{\alpha,i}}^{2}\|U^{\alpha}_{i,i}\|_{2}^{2}\\
&=\sum_{\alpha\in F}\sum_{i=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}\dim_{q}(\alpha)^{2}{\uprho_{\alpha,i}}^{2}\tfrac{1}{\dim_{q}(\alpha)\uprho_{\alpha,i}}=\sum_{\alpha\in F}\dim_{q}(\alpha)^{2}.\end{split}

For the lower bound, choose
\alpha\in F
and let
\chi_{\alpha}=\sum_{i=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}U^{\alpha}_{i,i}
be character of
\alpha
. Then
\|\chi_{\alpha}\|\leq\dim(\alpha)
and

\|p_{F}\|_{\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}=\|\omega_{F}\|\geq\bigl|\omega_{F}(\tfrac{\chi_{\alpha}}{\|\chi_{\alpha}\|})\bigr|=\tfrac{1}{\|\chi_{\alpha}\|}\bigl|\sum_{i=1}^{\dim(\alpha)}\dim_{q}(\alpha)\uprho_{\alpha,i}h(U^{\alpha*}_{i,i}\chi_{\alpha})\bigr|=\tfrac{\dim(\alpha)}{\|\chi_{\alpha}\|}\geq 1.

∎

The next lemma shows intuitively that one can correct an almost equality
a\approx\mathbbm{1}
over a finite set
F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
to an actual equality, with an error over which we have precise control.

Lemma 4.5
.

Let

\textstyle\mathbb{L}

be a discrete quantum group,
\varepsilon>0
,
\emptyset\neq F\subseteq\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
finite set and
a\in\M^{l}_{cb}(\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
. Assume that
\|\Theta^{l}(a)(x)-x\|\leq\varepsilon\|x\|
for
x\in\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Then there is
\tilde{a}\in\M^{l}_{cb}(\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
such that
\Theta^{l}(\tilde{a})(x)=x
for
x\in\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
,
\tilde{a}-a\in\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
and
\|\tilde{a}-a\|_{\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\leq\varepsilon\sum_{\alpha\in F}\dim_{q}(\alpha)^{2}
. If
a\in\mathcal{Z}\M^{l}_{cb}(\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
, then we can take
\tilde{a}\in\mathcal{Z}\M^{l}_{cb}(\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))
.

Proof.

Write
a=(a_{\alpha})_{\alpha\in\Irr(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}
. Define
b=\sum_{\alpha\in F}(p_{\alpha}-a_{\alpha})\in\mathrm{c}_{00}({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
and
\tilde{a}=a+b
. Since
\tilde{a}_{\alpha}=p_{\alpha}\,(\alpha\in F)
, we have
\Theta^{l}(\tilde{a})(x)=x
for
x\in\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Furthermore

\tilde{a}-a=b=\sum_{\alpha\in F}(p_{\alpha}-a_{\alpha})=(\mathbbm{1}-a)\sum_{\alpha\in F}p_{\alpha}=(\mathbbm{1}-a)p_{F}=(\mathbbm{1}-a)\widehat{\lambda}(\omega_{F})=\widehat{\lambda}\bigl(\Theta^{l}(\mathbbm{1}-a)_{*}(\omega_{F})\bigr).

Consequently, using the facts that
\Theta^{l}(p_{F})_{*}(\omega_{F})=\omega_{F}
,
\Theta^{l}(p_{F})(x)\in\Pol_{F}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
for
x\in\operatorname{L}^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
and
p_{F}
is central

\begin{split}&\quad\;\|\tilde{a}-a\|_{\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}=\|\Theta^{l}(\mathbbm{1}-a)_{*}(\omega_{F})\|=\sup_{x\in\LL^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}),\|x\|=1}\!\bigl|\langle x,\Theta^{l}(\mathbbm{1}-a)_{*}(\omega_{F})\rangle\bigr|\\
&=\sup_{x\in\LL^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}),\|x\|=1}\!\bigl|\langle x,\Theta^{l}(\mathbbm{1}-a)_{*}\Theta^{l}(p_{F})_{*}(\omega_{F})\rangle\bigr|=\sup_{x\in\LL^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}),\|x\|=1}\!\bigl|\langle\Theta^{l}(\mathbbm{1}-a)\Theta^{l}(p_{F})(x),\omega_{F}\rangle\bigr|\\
&\leq\sup_{x\in\LL^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}),\|x\|=1}\!\|\Theta^{l}(p_{F})(x)-\Theta^{l}(a)(\Theta^{l}(p_{F})(x))\|\|\omega_{F}\|\leq\sup_{x\in\LL^{\infty}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}),\|x\|=1}\!\varepsilon\|\Theta^{l}(p_{F})(x)\|\|\omega_{F}\|\\
&=\varepsilon\|\omega_{F}\|\|\Theta^{l}(p_{F})\|\leq\varepsilon\|p_{F}\|_{\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\|p_{F}\|_{cb}\leq\varepsilon\|p_{F}\|_{\A({\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}^{2},\end{split}

hence the first claim follows from Lemma
4.4
. If
a
is central, then so is
b
and consequently
\tilde{a}
.
∎

Let us remark that using
[
12
, Corollary 2.2.4]
one can obtain a better bound for
\|\tilde{a}-a\|_{cb}
– we will however not need this. Our main result, in the language of modules, is the following.

Proposition 4.6
.

Let
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1},{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}
be discrete quantum groups and
{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}={\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1}\times{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}
their product. Then

(4.2)

{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))={}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1}))\;{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}))

and

(4.3)

{}_{\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))={}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})}\Lambda_{cb,\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1}))\;{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}\Lambda_{cb,\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})).

Proof.

The easier inequality
\leq
was already established in
[
15
, Proposition 3.2]
(after conjunction with Theorem
3.5
), let us give an essentially equivalent argument for the convenience of the reader. Recall that
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})=\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})\otimes\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})
as
\mathrm{C}^{*}
-algebras and
\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})=\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})\widehat{\otimes}\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})
as completely contractive Banach algebras. It is enough to assume that both
{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1}))
and
{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}))
are finite, let
(\varphi_{\lambda})_{\lambda\in\Lambda}
and
(\psi_{\mu})_{\mu\in\Sigma}
be the corresponding maps. Then we can construct new net
(\varphi_{\lambda}\otimes\psi_{\mu})_{(\lambda,\mu)\in\Lambda\times\Sigma}
of finite rank maps in
{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})\widehat{\otimes}\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}\CB(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})\otimes\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}))
(
[
12
, Proposition 8.1.5]
). For any
\lambda,\mu
we have
\|\varphi_{\lambda}\otimes\psi_{\mu}\|_{cb}\leq{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1}))\,{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}))
and clearly
\varphi_{\lambda}\otimes\psi_{\mu}\xrightarrow[(\lambda,\mu)\in\Lambda\times\Sigma]{}\textnormal{id}
on a norm dense set
\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})\odot\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})\subseteq\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})
. Consequently
(\varphi_{\lambda}\otimes\psi_{\mu})_{(\lambda,\mu)\in\Lambda\times\Sigma}
converges to
id
in the point-norm topology. This allows us to conclude inequality
\leq
in (
4.2
). An analogous reasoning gives inequality
\leq
in (
4.3
): the only difference is that if
\varphi_{\lambda}
and
\psi_{\mu}
are bimodule maps, then so is
\varphi_{\lambda}\otimes\psi_{\mu}
.

Let us now prove the converse inequalities; we will treat both cases at the same time. Assume by contradiction that (
4.2
) or (
4.3
) does not hold, i.e.

{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))<{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1}))\;{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}\Lambda_{cb}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2}))

or

{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}\Lambda_{cb,\operatorname{L}^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}))<{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})}\Lambda_{cb,\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{1}))\;{}_{\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}\Lambda_{cb,\LL^{1}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})}(\mathrm{C}(\widehat{\mathchoice{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\displaystyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\textstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}{\raisebox{0.0pt}{\scalebox{1}[-1]{$\scriptscriptstyle\mathbb{L}$}}}}_{2})).

Then we can choose positive constants
C_{1},C_{2}
such that

(4.4)
